#### Q: Any other suspect is the murderer (False)

### Biconditional Audit (P if and only if Q)

#### P: Miss Peach is the murderer (True)
#### Q: The Revolver is in the Gazebo at 12:00 AM (True)

Both P and Q are True.

### Contradiction Search

1. **Motive Verification:**
   - Miss Peach is motivated by Greed.
   - The motive checks out as it does not directly contradict any other clues.

2. **Location and Time Verification:**
   - Miss Peach is in the Fountain at 12:00 AM.
   - At 12:00 AM, no other suspects are in the Fountain except Miss Peach and Sgt. Gray.
   - The Alone Test shows that Mr. Boddy was alone with Miss Peach in the room with the Rope, which fits the criteria.

3. **Weapon and Room Analysis:**
   - The Revolver is in the Gazebo at 12:00 AM.
   - The Rope is used as the murder weapon, and Miss Peach is the murderer.
   - The murder weapon (Rope) and the Revolver are confirmed to be in different rooms, which is consistent.

4. **Manhattan Falsification:**
   - Miss Peach moves from the Fountain (2,1) to the Studio (1,2) at 12:00 AM.
   - \[|2-1| + |1-2| = 2\]
   - Since 2 is greater than 1, this is a physical violation.

### Physical Violation Detection

The Manhattan distance check reveals that Miss Peach’s move from the Fountain (2,1) to the Studio (1,2) violates the physical rule of moving only orthogonally adjacent rooms (maximum distance of 1).

- Time: 12:00 AM

- Time: 12:00 AM

[33mAuditor[0m (to Agency):

### Handoff Phrase:
AUDITOR, PERFORM A HARD AUDIT ON THESE LOGIC BRANCHES. SEARCH FOR PHYSICAL AND LOGICAL CONTRADICTIONS.

### Analysis and Hard Audit:

#### Suspect: Miss Scarlet
- **Suspect Location at 12:00 AM:**
  - Clue: Miss Scarlet moved from the Study to the Gazebo at 12:00 AM.
  - Physical Path: Study -> Gazebo.
    - Math: |2-2| + |1-1| = 0
    - No violation.

- **Weapon Location at 12:00 AM:**
  - Clue: The Revolver was in the same room as the Lead Pipe at 12:00 AM (already satisfied by being in the Gazebo).
  - Clue: The Rope was in the same room as the Wrench at 12:00 AM.
    - Both weapons are in the Gazebo.
    - Math: |2-2| + |1-1| = 0
    - No violation.

- **Room of Murder:**
  - The Revolver was in the Gazebo at 12:00 AM if and only if the murder weapon was in the Billiard Room at 12:00 AM (Revolver is not the murder weapon, it's the Rope).
  - Since the Revolver is in the Gazebo, the murder weapon must be in the Billiard Room.
  - However, the Revolver was in the Gazebo at 12:00 AM.
  - This creates a contradiction as the Revolver cannot be both the murder weapon and in the Gazebo.

#### Physical Violations:
- **Contradiction Search:**
  - The murder weapon (Rope) is not in the Gazebo until 12:00 AM, but Clue states the revolver (not the murder weapon) is in the Gazebo at 12:00 AM.

#### Logical Contradictions:
- **Biconditional Audit:**
  - Revolver in the Gazebo at 12:00 AM if and only if the murder weapon was in the Billiard Room at 12:00 AM.
  - Since the revolver is in the Gazebo, the Billiard Room must have the murder weapon. Hence, the revolver cannot be the murder weapon. This is a logical contradiction.

### Final Rejection and Corrected A-H List:
Since the Detective's logic branches have been contradicted:

**Final Output:**

A. Miss Scarlet
B. Study
C. 12:00 AM
D. Betrayal
E. Gazebo
F. Study
G. Cloak Room
